# Review: the weapon enchant's element chosen in its QTE — Glyphbound (UE 5.9)

You are reviewing, read-only. Run `git diff HEAD -- Glyphbound/Source Glyphbound/Content/Data` and read around it in
`QTE/GBQTELogic.h/.cpp`, `QTE/GBQTEComponent.h/.cpp`, `Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.h/.cpp`, `UI/GBHUD.cpp`
(`DrawQTE`, `DrawQTEElementChoice`), `Player/GBPlayerCharacter.cpp` (how QTE input reaches `HandleKey`, the camera lock
and the held movement during the QTE), `Tests/GBQTETests.cpp` (new `Glyphbound.QTE.SessionChoice`) and the changed UI
smoke steps in `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp`. Ignore Devlog and GDD files.

What changed (owner, Nov 13): the weapon enchant's QTE starts with a new `Choice` phase. The spellbook passes fixed
choice keys (A / Left = fire, W / Up = lightning, D / Right = ice) through `StartQTE`; a key of the list picks that
option and part 1 starts; other keys are ignored; the step has its own clock (`QTE.ChoiceTimeLimit`, 3 s) and, when it
runs out, nothing is picked and part 1 starts. `FGBQTEResult::Choice` carries the pick; `HandleQTEFinished` sets the
enchant element from it before the cast is finished. The HUD shows three element tiles with their keys while choosing,
and the timer shows the choice time.

## What to look for (ranked)
1. Logic: the `Choice` value added at the end of `EGBQTEPhase` (anything that compares phases with < or >, or treats
   "not Sequence and not Gesture" as finished or inactive; `Advance` returning early; `IsFinished`; `CancelQTE` during
   the choice; `GetResult` power / reach / success when the choice timed out or the QTE was cancelled in the choice).
2. Input during the choice: the same keys (W / A / D) are part 1's keys; can one press both pick and count as a
   sequence step? Gamepad face buttons map to the same enum? Gesture input during the choice? The held movement and
   camera lock during a longer QTE (up to 3 s more).
3. Gameplay: the time dilation now lasts up to 3 s longer; anything timed to the QTE's old maximum length (AI,
   enchant cooldown starting, smoke steps, the spellbook's busy state, weapon switch lock).
4. The element applied when the cast fizzles or is cancelled; the QTE off path (no choice, element unchanged).
5. The new test: can it fail for a wrong implementation?

Do not suggest style rewrites or new features. For each finding give: file:line, what goes wrong, a concrete
scenario, and a fix. If you find nothing real, say so plainly.

codex
I’ll read the diff and trace the choice phase through input, cancellation, cast completion, and the tests. I’ll keep the review read-only and report concrete defects only.

 void UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleQTEFinished(const FGBQTEResult& Result, FName SpellId)
 {
+	// The element picked in the enchant's QTE becomes the enchant element (also for the next casts). Nothing picked
+	// (time ran out, or the QTE is off) keeps the element the player had.
+	if (SpellId == EnchantSpellId && Result.Choice >= 0 && Result.Choice < NumEnchantChoices)
+	{
+		SetEnchantElement(GetEnchantChoiceElement(Result.Choice));
+	}
 	FinishCast(SpellId, Result);
 }

+// The enchant's element choice (owner, Nov 13): a first step on fixed keys, with its own clock, that never counts
+// toward Power and never eats the time of part 1.
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBQTESessionChoiceTest, "Glyphbound.QTE.SessionChoice", EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBQTESessionChoiceTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	FGBQTEConfig Config = GBQTETest::MakeConfig();
+	Config.ChoiceKeys = { EGBQTEKey::Left, EGBQTEKey::Up, EGBQTEKey::Right };
+	Config.ChoiceTimeLimit = 2.f;
+
+	{
+		FRandomStream Random(11);
+		FGBQTESession Session;
+		Session.Start(Config, Random);
+		TestTrue(TEXT("Starts in the Choice phase"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Choice);
+		TestFalse(TEXT("A key that is not an option is ignored"), Session.PressKey(EGBQTEKey::Down));
+		TestTrue(TEXT("Still choosing after a non-option key"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Choice);
+		Session.Advance(1.5f);
+		TestTrue(TEXT("Still choosing before the choice limit"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Choice);
+		TestTrue(TEXT("The option key is taken"), Session.PressKey(EGBQTEKey::Up));
+		TestEqual(TEXT("Up is the second option"), Session.ChosenIndex, 1);
+		TestTrue(TEXT("Part 1 starts after the choice"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Sequence);
+		TestEqual(TEXT("The choice did not consume a step of part 1"), Session.SequenceResults.Num(), 0);
+		TestEqual(TEXT("The choice did not eat the QTE's own time"), Session.GetTimeRemaining(), Config.TimeLimit, 1e-4f);
+		for (const EGBQTEKey Key : TArray<EGBQTEKey>(Session.Sequence))
+		{
+			Session.PressKey(Key);
+		}
+		const FGBQTEResult Result = Session.GetResult();
+		TestEqual(TEXT("The result carries the choice"), Result.Choice, 1);
+		TestEqual(TEXT("Power counts part 1 only: all four right"), Result.Power, 1.f, 1e-4f);
+	}
+	{
+		FRandomStream Random(11);
+		FGBQTESession Session;
+		Session.Start(Config, Random);
+		Session.Advance(2.1f);
+		TestTrue(TEXT("Choice time out: part 1 starts"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Sequence);
+		TestEqual(TEXT("Nothing picked"), Session.ChosenIndex, -1);
+		Session.PressKey(EGBQTEKey::Left);
+		TestEqual(TEXT("A late option key is part 1's input now"), Session.SequenceResults.Num(), 1);
+		TestEqual(TEXT("and not a choice"), Session.ChosenIndex, -1);
+		TestEqual(TEXT("No choice in the result"), Session.GetResult().Choice, -1);
+	}
+	{
+		FRandomStream Random(11);
+		FGBQTESession Session;
+		Session.Start(GBQTETest::MakeConfig(), Random);
+		TestTrue(TEXT("No options: no Choice step"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Sequence);
+	}
+	return true;
+}

 #endif // WITH_DEV_AUTOMATION_TESTS

 // ======================================================================== QTE (GDD 7)
 
+void AGBHUD::DrawQTEElementChoice(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, const FGBQTESession& Session, EGBHudSkin Skin)
+{
+	const float S = UIScale();
+	const float P = ArtPixel();
+	const float W = Canvas->ClipX;
+	const float H = Canvas->ClipY;
+	const float CX = W * 0.5f;
+	const bool bCyber = Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk;
+	const UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook();
+	const EGBElement Current = Spellbook ? Spellbook->GetEnchantElement() : EGBElement::Fire;
+	const int32 Count = Session.Config.ChoiceKeys.Num();
+	const float TileW = bCyber ? FMath::RoundToFloat(150.f * S) : FMath::RoundToFloat(40.f * P / P) * P;
+	const float TileH = bCyber ? FMath::RoundToFloat(120.f * S) : 32.f * P;
+	const float Gap = bCyber ? FMath::RoundToFloat(24.f * S) : 6.f * P;
+	const float RowY = FMath::RoundToFloat(H * 0.255f / P) * P;
+	const float X0 = FMath::RoundToFloat((CX - (Count * TileW + FMath::Max(0, Count - 1) * Gap) * 0.5f) / P) * P;
+	for (int32 i = 0; i < Count; ++i)
+	{
+		const EGBElement Element = UGBSpellbookComponent::GetEnchantChoiceElement(i);
+		const FLinearColor EC = GBHudText::ElementColor(Element);
+		const bool bCurrent = Element == Current;
+		// The key that picks it sits where it is on the keyboard: W (the middle option) a little higher than A and D.
+		const float X = X0 + i * (TileW + Gap);
+		const float Y = RowY + (Session.Config.ChoiceKeys[i] == EGBQTEKey::Up ? 0.f : (bCyber ? 18.f * S : 4.f * P));
+		const FString Letter = GBHudLocal::QTEKeyLetter(Session.Config.ChoiceKeys[i]);
+		const FString Name = GBHudText::ElementName(Element).ToUpper();
+		if (bCyber)
+		{
+			DrawGlow(X + TileW * 0.5f, Y + TileH * 0.5f, TileW * 0.8f, TileH * 0.8f, GBAlpha(EC, 0.18f));
+			CyBox(X, Y, TileW, TileH, 14.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.85f), bCurrent ? GBCy::Pale : EC, 0x5, bCurrent ? 2.f : 1.f);
+			FillRect(X + 12.f * S, Y + TileH - 8.f * S, TileW - 24.f * S, 3.f * S, EC);
+			FCyTextStyle Key;
+			Key.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
+			Key.Px = 44.f;
+			Key.Color = EC;
+			DrawCyText(Letter, X + TileW * 0.5f, Y + 10.f * S, Key, 0.5f);
+			FCyTextStyle Label;
+			Label.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
+			Label.Px = 20.f;
+			Label.Spacing = 140;
+			Label.bUpper = true;
+			Label.Color = GBCy::Pale;
+			DrawCyText(Name, X + TileW * 0.5f, Y + 68.f * S, Label, 0.5f);
+			if (bCurrent)
+			{
+				FCyTextStyle Tag;
+				Tag.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
+				Tag.Px = 11.f;
+				Tag.Color = GBCy::Pale;
+				DrawCyText(TEXT("CURRENT"), X + TileW * 0.5f, Y - 18.f * S, Tag, 0.5f);
+			}
+		}
+		else
+		{
+			DrawGlow(X + TileW * 0.5f, Y + TileH * 0.5f, TileW * 0.8f, TileH * 0.8f, GBAlpha(EC, 0.2f));
+			FillRect(X, Y, TileW, TileH, GBRpg::StoneDeep);
+			RpgStone(X, Y, TileW, TileH, FLinearColor(0.8f, 0.8f, 0.9f, 0.6f));
+			RpgFrame(X, Y, TileW, TileH, bCurrent ? ERpgFrame::Gold : ERpgFrame::Iron);
+			RpgGem(X + TileW * 0.5f, Y + 9.f * P, EC, true);
+			FPixelTextStyle Key;
+			Key.Scale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P));
+			Key.Ink = &GBRpg::InkBone;
+			Key.Outline = GBRpg::Outline;
+			DrawPixelText(Letter, X + TileW * 0.5f, Y + 14.f * P, Key, 0.5f);
+			FPixelTextStyle Label;
+			Label.Scale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
+			Label.Color = EC;
+			Label.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
+			DrawPixelText(Name, X + TileW * 0.5f, Y + TileH - 7.f * P, Label, 0.5f);
+			if (bCurrent)
+			{
+				FPixelTextStyle Tag;
+				Tag.Scale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
+				Tag.Ink = &GBRpg::InkGold;
+				Tag.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
+				DrawPixelText(TEXT("CURRENT"), X + TileW * 0.5f, Y - 6.f * P, Tag, 0.5f);
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body, EGBTextTone::Normal, TEXT("Choose the element"), CX, RowY + TileH + (bCyber ? 34.f * S : 9.f * P), 0.5f);
+}

Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:34: float FGBGestureRecognizer::PathLength(TConstArrayView<FVector2D> Points)
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:35: {
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:36: 	double Length = 0.0;

Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:40: 	}
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:41: 	return static_cast<float>(Length);

Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:44: float FGBGestureRecognizer::TotalTurnDegrees(TConstArrayView<FVector2D> Points)
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:45: {
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:46: 	double TotalRadians = 0.0;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:47: 	bool bHasPrevious = false;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:48: 	FVector2D Previous = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:49: 	for (int32 i = 1; i < Points.Num(); ++i)
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:50: 	{
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:51: 		const FVector2D Segment = Points[i] - Points[i - 1];
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:52: 		if (Segment.Size() < GBQTELogicPrivate::MinSegmentLength)
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:53: 		{
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:54: 			continue;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:55: 		}
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:56: 		if (bHasPrevious)
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:57: 		{
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:58: 			// With +Y down, a positive cross product is a clockwise turn as seen on screen.
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:59: 			const double Cross = Previous.X * Segment.Y - Previous.Y * Segment.X;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:60: 			const double Dot = Previous.X * Segment.X + Previous.Y * Segment.Y;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:61: 			TotalRadians += FMath::Atan2(Cross, Dot);
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:62: 		}
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:63: 		Previous = Segment;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:64: 		bHasPrevious = true;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:65: 	}
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:66: 	return static_cast<float>(FMath::RadiansToDegrees(TotalRadians));
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:67: }
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:68: 
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:69: float FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(TConstArrayView<FVector2D> Points, EGBGesture Gesture, float MinLength)
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:70: {
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:71: 	if (Points.Num() < 2)
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:72: 	{
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:73: 		return 0.f;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:74: 	}
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:75: 	const float Length = PathLength(Points);
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:76: 	if (Length < MinLength || Length < GBQTELogicPrivate::MinSegmentLength)
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:77: 	{
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:78: 		return 0.f;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:79: 	}
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:80: 
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:81: 	const FVector2D Start = Points[0];
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:82: 	const FVector2D End = Points.Last();
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:83: 
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:84: 	if (!GBQTELogicPrivate::IsCircle(Gesture))
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:85: 	{
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:86: 		const FVector2D Chord = End - Start;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:87: 		const double ChordLength = Chord.Size();
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:88: 		if (ChordLength < GBQTELogicPrivate::MinSegmentLength)
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:89: 		{
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:90: 			return 0.f;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:91: 		}
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:92: 		const double Straightness = FMath::Clamp(ChordLength / Length, 0.0, 1.0);
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:93: 		const double DirectionMatch = FMath::Clamp(FVector2D::DotProduct(Chord / ChordLength, GBQTELogicPrivate::LineDirection(Gesture)), 0.0, 1.0);
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:94: 		return static_cast<float>(Straightness * DirectionMatch);
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:95: 	}
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:96: 
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:97: 	const float Sign = Gesture == EGBGesture::CircleClockwise ? 1.f : -1.f;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:98: 	const float Turn = TotalTurnDegrees(Points) * Sign;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:99: 	const float TurnScore = FMath::Clamp(Turn / 360.f, 0.f, 1.f);
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:100: 	if (TurnScore <= 0.f)
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:101: 	{
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:102: 		return 0.f;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:103: 	}
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:104: 
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:105: 	// Closure: 1 when the stroke ends where it began, 0 when the gap is as large as the stroke's bounding box diagonal.
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:106: 	FVector2D Min = Start;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:107: 	FVector2D Max = Start;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:108: 	for (const FVector2D& P : Points)
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:109: 	{
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:110: 		Min = FVector2D::Min(Min, P);
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:111: 		Max = FVector2D::Max(Max, P);
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:112: 	}
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:113: 	const double Size = (Max - Min).Size();
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:114: 	if (Size < GBQTELogicPrivate::MinSegmentLength)
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:115: 	{
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:116: 		return 0.f;
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:117: 	}
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:118: 	const double Gap = FVector2D::Distance(Start, End);
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:119: 	const double Closure = FMath::Clamp(1.0 - Gap / Size, 0.0, 1.0);
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:120: 	return static_cast<float>(TurnScore * Closure);
Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:121: }

Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTELogic.cpp:250: 	}

74: FGBQTEConfig UGBQTEComponent::BuildConfig() const
75: {
76: 	const FGBQTEConfig Defaults;
77: 	FGBQTEConfig Config;
78: 	Config.SequenceLength = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(ReadStat(GBStat::QTESequenceLength, static_cast<float>(Defaults.SequenceLength))));
79: 	Config.GestureCount = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(ReadStat(GBStat::QTEGestureCount, static_cast<float>(Defaults.GestureCount))));
80: 	Config.TimeLimit = ReadStat(GBStat::QTETimeLimit, Defaults.TimeLimit);
81: 	Config.GestureMinScore = ReadStat(GBQTEStat::GestureMinScore, Defaults.GestureMinScore);
82: 	Config.GestureMinLength = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadStat(GBQTEStat::GestureMinLength, Defaults.GestureMinLength));
83: 
84: 	if (const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
85: 	{
86: 		const UEnum* GestureEnum = StaticEnum<EGBGesture>();
87: 		const int64 MaxGesture = static_cast<int64>(EGBGesture::CircleCounterClockwise);
88: 		for (const FString& Name : Data->GetQTE().Gestures)
89: 		{
90: 			const int64 Value = GestureEnum->GetValueByNameString(Name);
91: 			if (Value == INDEX_NONE || Value < 0 || Value > MaxGesture)
92: 			{
93: 				UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("QTE: unknown gesture '%s' in QTE.json; ignored."), *Name);
94: 				continue;
95: 			}
96: 			Config.AllowedGestures.AddUnique(static_cast<EGBGesture>(Value));
97: 		}
98: 	}
99: 	return Config;
100: }

102: bool UGBQTEComponent::StartQTE(FName SpellId, FGBOnQTEFinished OnFinished, const TArray<EGBQTEKey>& ChoiceKeys)
103: {
104: 	if (bActive)
105: 	{
106: 		return false;
107: 	}
108: 
109: 	if (!IsEnabled())
110: 	{
111: 		FGBQTEResult Result;
112: 		Result.Power = 0.f;
113: 		Result.Reach = 0.f;
114: 		Result.bUsedQTE = false;
115: 		Result.bSuccess = true;
116: 		LastResult = Result;
117: 		OnFinished.ExecuteIfBound(Result);
118: 		return true;
119: 	}
120: 
121: 	const float TimeDilation = ReadStat(GBStat::QTETimeDilation, GBQTEComponentPrivate::DefaultTimeDilation);
122: 	GestureIdleSubmitTime = ReadStat(GBQTEStat::GestureIdleSubmitTime, GBQTEComponentPrivate::DefaultGestureIdleSubmitTime);
123: 	LineSubmitLengthMult = ReadStat(GBQTEStat::LineSubmitLengthMult, GBQTEComponentPrivate::DefaultLineSubmitLengthMult);
124: 
125: 	Session = FGBQTESession();
126: 	FGBQTEConfig Config = BuildConfig();
127: 	Config.ChoiceKeys = ChoiceKeys;
128: 	Config.ChoiceTimeLimit = FMath::Max(0.1f, ReadStat(GBQTEStat::ChoiceTimeLimit, Config.ChoiceTimeLimit));
129: 	Session.Start(Config, Random);
130: 	ActiveSpellId = SpellId;
131: 	FinishedDelegate = OnFinished;
132: 	GestureIdleTime = 0.f;
133: 	bActive = true;
134: 
135: 	if (Session.IsFinished())
136: 	{
137: 		// No steps configured: nothing to play.
138: 		Finish();
139: 		return true;
140: 	}
141: 
142: 	UGameplayStatics::SetGlobalTimeDilation(this, TimeDilation);
143: 	SetComponentTickEnabled(true);
144: 	return true;
145: }
146: 
147: void UGBQTEComponent::CancelQTE()
148: {
149: 	if (!bActive)
150: 	{
151: 		return;
152: 	}
153: 	Session.Phase = EGBQTEPhase::Finished;
154: 	// A cancelled QTE never casts: the result is unsuccessful even if some steps were right.
155: 	bForceFailResult = true;
156: 	Finish();
157: }
158: 
159: void UGBQTEComponent::HandleKey(EGBQTEKey Key)
160: {
161: 	if (!bActive)
162: 	{
163: 		return;
164: 	}
165: 	Session.PressKey(Key);
166: 	if (Session.IsFinished())
167: 	{
168: 		Finish();
169: 	}
170: }
171: 
172: void UGBQTEComponent::HandleGestureInput(const FVector2D& Delta)
173: {
174: 	if (!bActive || Session.Phase != EGBQTEPhase::Gesture || Delta.IsNearlyZero())
175: 	{
176: 		return;
177: 	}
178: 	Session.AddGestureDelta(Delta);
179: 	GestureIdleTime = 0.f;
180: }
181: 
182: void UGBQTEComponent::TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction)
183: {
184: 	Super::TickComponent(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);
185: 
186: 	if (!bActive)
187: 	{
188: 		return;
189: 	}
190: 
191: 	// DeltaTime is dilated by the QTE slow-down; the QTE runs on the undilated frame time.
192: 	const float RealDelta = static_cast<float>(FApp::GetDeltaTime());
193: 	Session.Advance(RealDelta);
194: 
195: 	if (Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Gesture)
196: 	{
197: 		GestureIdleTime += RealDelta;
198: 		if (Session.IsGestureReadyToJudge() && Session.Gestures.IsValidIndex(Session.GestureScores.Num()))
199: 		{
200: 			const EGBGesture Wanted = Session.Gestures[Session.GestureScores.Num()];
201: 			const bool bCircle = Wanted == EGBGesture::CircleClockwise || Wanted == EGBGesture::CircleCounterClockwise;
202: 
203: 			bool bSubmit = GestureIdleTime >= GestureIdleSubmitTime;
204: 			if (bCircle)
205: 			{
206: 				bSubmit |= FMath::Abs(FGBGestureRecognizer::TotalTurnDegrees(Session.CurrentStroke)) >= GBQTEComponentPrivate::FullCircleDegrees;
207: 			}
208: 			else
209: 			{
210: 				bSubmit |= FGBGestureRecognizer::PathLength(Session.CurrentStroke) >= LineSubmitLengthMult * Session.Config.GestureMinLength;
211: 			}
212: 
213: 			if (bSubmit)
214: 			{
215: 				Session.SubmitGesture();
216: 				GestureIdleTime = 0.f;
217: 			}
218: 		}
219: 	}
220: 
221: 	if (Session.IsFinished())
222: 	{
223: 		Finish();
224: 	}
225: }
226: 
227: void UGBQTEComponent::Finish()
228: {
229: 	if (!bActive)
230: 	{
231: 		return;
232: 	}
233: 	UGameplayStatics::SetGlobalTimeDilation(this, 1.f);
234: 	bActive = false;
235: 	SetComponentTickEnabled(false);
236: 	FGBQTEResult Result = Session.GetResult();
237: 	if (bForceFailResult)
238: 	{
239: 		Result.bSuccess = false;
240: 		Result.bUsedQTE = true;
241: 	}
242: 	bForceFailResult = false;
243: 	LastResult = Result;
244: 	ActiveSpellId = NAME_None;
245: 
246: 	// Copy first: the callback may start a new QTE, which overwrites FinishedDelegate (and LastResult).
247: 	FGBOnQTEFinished Delegate = FinishedDelegate;
248: 	FinishedDelegate.Unbind();
249: 	Delegate.ExecuteIfBound(Result);
250: }
251: 
252: void UGBQTEComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
253: {
254: 	if (bActive)
255: 	{
256: 		UGameplayStatics::SetGlobalTimeDilation(this, 1.f);
257: 		bActive = false;
258: 		FinishedDelegate.Unbind();
259: 	}
260: 	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
261: }

124: // FGBQTESession
125: 
126: void FGBQTESession::Start(const FGBQTEConfig& InConfig, FRandomStream& Random)
127: {
128: 	Config = InConfig;
129: 	Config.SequenceLength = FMath::Max(0, Config.SequenceLength);
130: 	Config.GestureCount = FMath::Max(0, Config.GestureCount);
131: 
132: 	Sequence.Reset();
133: 	SequenceResults.Reset();
134: 	Gestures.Reset();
135: 	GestureScores.Reset();
136: 	CurrentStroke.Reset();
137: 	TimeElapsed = 0.f;
138: 	bTimedOut = false;
139: 	ChosenIndex = -1;
140: 	ChoiceElapsed = 0.f;
141: 
142: 	for (int32 i = 0; i < Config.SequenceLength; ++i)
143: 	{
144: 		Sequence.Add(static_cast<EGBQTEKey>(Random.RandRange(0, GBQTELogicPrivate::NumKeys - 1)));
145: 	}
146: 
147: 	TArray<EGBGesture> Pool = Config.AllowedGestures;
148: 	if (Pool.Num() == 0)
149: 	{
150: 		Pool.Append(GBQTELogicPrivate::AllGestures, UE_ARRAY_COUNT(GBQTELogicPrivate::AllGestures));
151: 	}
152: 	for (int32 i = 0; i < Config.GestureCount; ++i)
153: 	{
154: 		Gestures.Add(Pool[Random.RandRange(0, Pool.Num() - 1)]);
155: 	}
156: 
157: 	Phase = Config.ChoiceKeys.Num() > 0 ? EGBQTEPhase::Choice : FirstStepPhase();
158: }
159: 
160: EGBQTEPhase FGBQTESession::FirstStepPhase() const
161: {
162: 	if (Sequence.Num() > 0)
163: 	{
164: 		return EGBQTEPhase::Sequence;
165: 	}
166: 	return Gestures.Num() > 0 ? EGBQTEPhase::Gesture : EGBQTEPhase::Finished;
167: }
168: 
169: bool FGBQTESession::PressKey(EGBQTEKey Key)
170: {
171: 	if (Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Choice)
172: 	{
173: 		const int32 Index = Config.ChoiceKeys.Find(Key);
174: 		if (Index == INDEX_NONE)
175: 		{
176: 			return false; // not an option: ignored, the step waits
177: 		}
178: 		ChosenIndex = Index;
179: 		Phase = FirstStepPhase();
180: 		return true;
181: 	}
182: 	if (Phase != EGBQTEPhase::Sequence || !Sequence.IsValidIndex(SequenceResults.Num()))
183: 	{
184: 		return false;
185: 	}
186: 	const bool bRight = Sequence[SequenceResults.Num()] == Key;
187: 	SequenceResults.Add(bRight);
188: 	if (SequenceResults.Num() >= Sequence.Num())
189: 	{
190: 		CurrentStroke.Reset();
191: 		Phase = Gestures.Num() > 0 ? EGBQTEPhase::Gesture : EGBQTEPhase::Finished;
192: 	}
193: 	return bRight;
194: }
195: 
196: void FGBQTESession::AddGestureDelta(const FVector2D& Delta)
197: {
198: 	if (Phase != EGBQTEPhase::Gesture || Delta.IsNearlyZero())
199: 	{
200: 		return;
201: 	}
202: 	if (CurrentStroke.Num() == 0)
203: 	{
204: 		CurrentStroke.Add(FVector2D::ZeroVector);
205: 	}
206: 	CurrentStroke.Add(CurrentStroke.Last() + Delta);
207: }
208: 
209: float FGBQTESession::SubmitGesture()
210: {
211: 	if (Phase != EGBQTEPhase::Gesture || !Gestures.IsValidIndex(GestureScores.Num()))
212: 	{
213: 		return 0.f;
214: 	}
215: 	float GestureScore = FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(CurrentStroke, Gestures[GestureScores.Num()], Config.GestureMinLength);
216: 	if (GestureScore < Config.GestureMinScore)
217: 	{
218: 		GestureScore = 0.f;
219: 	}
220: 	GestureScores.Add(GestureScore);
221: 	CurrentStroke.Reset();
222: 	if (GestureScores.Num() >= Gestures.Num())
223: 	{
224: 		Phase = EGBQTEPhase::Finished;
225: 	}
226: 	return GestureScore;
227: }
228: 
229: bool FGBQTESession::IsGestureReadyToJudge() const
230: {
231: 	return Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Gesture && CurrentStroke.Num() >= 2
232: 		&& FGBGestureRecognizer::PathLength(CurrentStroke) >= Config.GestureMinLength;
233: }
234: 
235: void FGBQTESession::Advance(float RealDeltaSeconds)
236: {
237: 	if (Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Choice)
238: 	{
239: 		// The Choice step has its own clock; the QTE's own limit starts when it ends.
240: 		ChoiceElapsed += FMath::Max(0.f, RealDeltaSeconds);
241: 		if (ChoiceElapsed >= Config.ChoiceTimeLimit)
242: 		{
243: 			Phase = FirstStepPhase();
244: 		}
245: 		return;
246: 	}
247: 	if (Phase != EGBQTEPhase::Sequence && Phase != EGBQTEPhase::Gesture)
248: 	{
249: 		return;
250: 	}
251: 	TimeElapsed += FMath::Max(0.f, RealDeltaSeconds);
252: 	if (TimeElapsed >= Config.TimeLimit)
253: 	{
254: 		bTimedOut = true;
255: 		Phase = EGBQTEPhase::Finished;
256: 	}
257: }
258: 
259: FGBQTEResult FGBQTESession::GetResult() const
260: {
261: 	FGBQTEResult Result;
262: 	Result.bUsedQTE = true;
263: 	Result.bTimedOut = bTimedOut;
264: 	Result.Choice = ChosenIndex;
265: 
266: 	int32 RightKeys = 0;
267: 	for (bool bRight : SequenceResults)
268: 	{
269: 		RightKeys += bRight ? 1 : 0;
270: 	}
271: 
272: 	float ScoreSum = 0.f;
273: 	bool bAnyGesture = false;
274: 	for (float GestureScore : GestureScores)
275: 	{
276: 		ScoreSum += GestureScore;
277: 		bAnyGesture |= GestureScore > 0.f;
278: 	}
279: 
280: 	// A part with no steps does not weaken the spell (gesture-only or sequence-only setups).
281: 	Result.Power = Sequence.Num() > 0 ? static_cast<float>(RightKeys) / Sequence.Num() : 1.f;
282: 	Result.Reach = Gestures.Num() > 0 ? ScoreSum / Gestures.Num() : 1.f;
283: 
284: 	// GDD 7: the spell fails only if every step is missed (no steps = nothing missed).
285: 	const bool bNoSteps = Sequence.Num() == 0 && Gestures.Num() == 0;
286: 	Result.bSuccess = bNoSteps || RightKeys > 0 || bAnyGesture;
287: 	return Result;
288: }
// Pure-logic tests of the spell QTE (GDD 7): gesture scoring and the session state machine.
#include "QTE/GBQTELogic.h"
#include "Misc/AutomationTest.h"
#include "Algo/Reverse.h"

#if WITH_DEV_AUTOMATION_TESTS

namespace GBQTETest
{
	FGBQTEConfig MakeConfig()
	{
		FGBQTEConfig Config;
		Config.SequenceLength = 4;
		Config.GestureCount = 1;
		Config.TimeLimit = 3.f;
		Config.GestureMinScore = 0.35f;
		Config.GestureMinLength = 120.f;
		Config.AllowedGestures = { EGBGesture::LineRight };
		return Config;
	}

	EGBQTEKey WrongKey(EGBQTEKey Key)
	{
		return static_cast<EGBQTEKey>((static_cast<uint8>(Key) + 1) % 4);
	}

	/** Circle of radius R sampled in Segments steps; with +Y down, increasing angle is clockwise on screen. */
	TArray<FVector2D> ClockwiseCircle(double Radius, int32 Segments)
	{
		TArray<FVector2D> Points;
		for (int32 i = 0; i <= Segments; ++i)
		{
			const double Angle = 2.0 * UE_DOUBLE_PI * i / Segments;
			Points.Add(FVector2D(FMath::Cos(Angle), FMath::Sin(Angle)) * Radius);
		}
		return Points;
	}

	void DrawRight(FGBQTESession& Session, double Sign)
	{
		Session.AddGestureDelta(FVector2D(100.0 * Sign, 0.0));
		Session.AddGestureDelta(FVector2D(100.0 * Sign, 0.0));
	}
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBQTEGestureLineTest, "Glyphbound.QTE.GestureLine", EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBQTEGestureLineTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	const TArray<FVector2D> Straight = { {0, 0}, {50, 0}, {100, 0}, {200, 0} };
	TestTrue(TEXT("Straight right stroke scores high for LineRight"), FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(Straight, EGBGesture::LineRight, 120.f) > 0.9f);
	TestEqual(TEXT("Opposite direction scores 0"), FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(Straight, EGBGesture::LineLeft, 120.f), 0.f);
	TestEqual(TEXT("Perpendicular direction scores 0"), FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(Straight, EGBGesture::LineDown, 120.f), 0.f);

	const TArray<FVector2D> Wobbly = { {0, 0}, {50, 40}, {100, -40}, {150, 40}, {200, 0} };
	const float WobblyScore = FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(Wobbly, EGBGesture::LineRight, 120.f);
	TestTrue(TEXT("Wobbly right stroke scores between 0 and a straight one"), WobblyScore > 0.f && WobblyScore < 0.9f);

	const TArray<FVector2D> Up = { {0, 0}, {0, -200} };
	TestTrue(TEXT("Stroke towards -Y is LineUp"), FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(Up, EGBGesture::LineUp, 120.f) > 0.9f);
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBQTEGestureCircleTest, "Glyphbound.QTE.GestureCircle", EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBQTEGestureCircleTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	const TArray<FVector2D> Clockwise = GBQTETest::ClockwiseCircle(100.0, 32);
	TestTrue(TEXT("Turn of a clockwise circle is positive"), FGBGestureRecognizer::TotalTurnDegrees(Clockwise) > 300.f);
	TestTrue(TEXT("Clockwise circle scores high for CircleClockwise"), FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(Clockwise, EGBGesture::CircleClockwise, 120.f) > 0.8f);
	TestEqual(TEXT("Clockwise circle scores 0 for CircleCounterClockwise"), FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(Clockwise, EGBGesture::CircleCounterClockwise, 120.f), 0.f);

	TArray<FVector2D> CounterClockwise = Clockwise;
	Algo::Reverse(CounterClockwise);
	TestTrue(TEXT("Reversed circle scores high for CircleCounterClockwise"), FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(CounterClockwise, EGBGesture::CircleCounterClockwise, 120.f) > 0.8f);

	const TArray<FVector2D> Line = { {0, 0}, {300, 0} };
	TestEqual(TEXT("A straight line is not a circle"), FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(Line, EGBGesture::CircleClockwise, 120.f), 0.f);
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBQTEGestureShortTest, "Glyphbound.QTE.GestureShort", EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBQTEGestureShortTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	const TArray<FVector2D> Short = { {0, 0}, {50, 0} };
	TestEqual(TEXT("A stroke shorter than MinLength scores 0"), FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(Short, EGBGesture::LineRight, 120.f), 0.f);
	TestTrue(TEXT("The same stroke counts once MinLength allows it"), FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(Short, EGBGesture::LineRight, 40.f) > 0.9f);
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBQTESessionScoreTest, "Glyphbound.QTE.SessionScore", EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBQTESessionScoreTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	FRandomStream Random(1234);

	{
		FGBQTESession Session;
		Session.Start(GBQTETest::MakeConfig(), Random);
		TestEqual(TEXT("Sequence has SequenceLength keys"), Session.Sequence.Num(), 4);
		TestTrue(TEXT("Starts in the Sequence phase"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Sequence);
		for (EGBQTEKey Key : TArray<EGBQTEKey>(Session.Sequence))
		{
			TestTrue(TEXT("Right key is reported right"), Session.PressKey(Key));
		}
		TestTrue(TEXT("Gesture phase after the last key"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Gesture);
		GBQTETest::DrawRight(Session, 1.0);
		TestTrue(TEXT("Stroke is ready to judge"), Session.IsGestureReadyToJudge());
		Session.SubmitGesture();
		TestTrue(TEXT("Finished after the last gesture"), Session.IsFinished());
		const FGBQTEResult Result = Session.GetResult();
		TestEqual(TEXT("All right keys: Power 1"), Result.Power, 1.f);
		TestTrue(TEXT("Good gesture: Reach high"), Result.Reach > 0.9f);
		TestTrue(TEXT("Success"), Result.bSuccess && Result.bUsedQTE && !Result.bTimedOut);
	}

	{
		FGBQTESession Session;
		Session.Start(GBQTETest::MakeConfig(), Random);
		const TArray<EGBQTEKey> Sequence = Session.Sequence;
		TestFalse(TEXT("Wrong key is reported wrong"), Session.PressKey(GBQTETest::WrongKey(Sequence[0])));
		for (int32 i = 1; i < Sequence.Num(); ++i)
		{
			Session.PressKey(Sequence[i]);
		}
		GBQTETest::DrawRight(Session, 1.0);
		Session.SubmitGesture();
		TestEqual(TEXT("One wrong key of four: Power 0.75"), Session.GetResult().Power, 0.75f, 1e-4f);
	}

	{
		FGBQTEConfig Config = GBQTETest::MakeConfig();
		Config.SequenceLength = 0;
		FGBQTESession Session;
		Session.Start(Config, Random);
		TestTrue(TEXT("No sequence: starts in the Gesture phase"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Gesture);
		GBQTETest::DrawRight(Session, 1.0);
		Session.SubmitGesture();
		TestEqual(TEXT("No sequence: Power counts as 1"), Session.GetResult().Power, 1.f);
	}
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBQTESessionAllMissedTest, "Glyphbound.QTE.SessionAllMissed", EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBQTESessionAllMissedTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	FRandomStream Random(99);
	FGBQTESession Session;
	Session.Start(GBQTETest::MakeConfig(), Random);
	for (EGBQTEKey Key : TArray<EGBQTEKey>(Session.Sequence))
	{
		Session.PressKey(GBQTETest::WrongKey(Key));
	}
	GBQTETest::DrawRight(Session, -1.0);
	TestEqual(TEXT("Opposite stroke scores 0"), Session.SubmitGesture(), 0.f);
	TestTrue(TEXT("Finished"), Session.IsFinished());
	const FGBQTEResult Result = Session.GetResult();
	TestEqual(TEXT("Power 0"), Result.Power, 0.f);
	TestEqual(TEXT("Reach 0"), Result.Reach, 0.f);
	TestFalse(TEXT("Every step missed: the spell fails"), Result.bSuccess);
	return true;
}

IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBQTESessionTimeoutTest, "Glyphbound.QTE.SessionTimeout", EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBQTESessionTimeoutTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	FRandomStream Random(7);
	FGBQTESession Session;
	Session.Start(GBQTETest::MakeConfig(), Random);
	Session.PressKey(Session.Sequence[0]);

	Session.Advance(1.f);
	TestFalse(TEXT("Not finished before the limit"), Session.IsFinished());
	Session.Advance(2.5f);
	TestTrue(TEXT("Finished at the limit"), Session.IsFinished());
	TestTrue(TEXT("Timed out"), Session.bTimedOut);
	TestFalse(TEXT("Keys are ignored after the end"), Session.PressKey(Session.Sequence[1]));

	const FGBQTEResult Result = Session.GetResult();
	TestTrue(TEXT("Result says timed out"), Result.bTimedOut);
	TestEqual(TEXT("Unpressed keys count as missed: Power 0.25"), Result.Power, 0.25f, 1e-4f);
	TestEqual(TEXT("Undrawn gesture counts as missed: Reach 0"), Result.Reach, 0.f);
	TestTrue(TEXT("One right key is enough to cast"), Result.bSuccess);
	return true;
}

// The enchant's element choice (owner, Nov 13): a first step on fixed keys, with its own clock, that never counts
// toward Power and never eats the time of part 1.
IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBQTESessionChoiceTest, "Glyphbound.QTE.SessionChoice", EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
bool FGBQTESessionChoiceTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
{
	FGBQTEConfig Config = GBQTETest::MakeConfig();
	Config.ChoiceKeys = { EGBQTEKey::Left, EGBQTEKey::Up, EGBQTEKey::Right };
	Config.ChoiceTimeLimit = 2.f;

	{
		FRandomStream Random(11);
		FGBQTESession Session;
		Session.Start(Config, Random);
		TestTrue(TEXT("Starts in the Choice phase"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Choice);
		TestFalse(TEXT("A key that is not an option is ignored"), Session.PressKey(EGBQTEKey::Down));
		TestTrue(TEXT("Still choosing after a non-option key"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Choice);
		Session.Advance(1.5f);
		TestTrue(TEXT("Still choosing before the choice limit"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Choice);
		TestTrue(TEXT("The option key is taken"), Session.PressKey(EGBQTEKey::Up));
		TestEqual(TEXT("Up is the second option"), Session.ChosenIndex, 1);
		TestTrue(TEXT("Part 1 starts after the choice"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Sequence);
		TestEqual(TEXT("The choice did not consume a step of part 1"), Session.SequenceResults.Num(), 0);
		TestEqual(TEXT("The choice did not eat the QTE's own time"), Session.GetTimeRemaining(), Config.TimeLimit, 1e-4f);
		for (const EGBQTEKey Key : TArray<EGBQTEKey>(Session.Sequence))
		{
			Session.PressKey(Key);
		}
		const FGBQTEResult Result = Session.GetResult();
		TestEqual(TEXT("The result carries the choice"), Result.Choice, 1);
		TestEqual(TEXT("Power counts part 1 only: all four right"), Result.Power, 1.f, 1e-4f);
	}
	{
		FRandomStream Random(11);
		FGBQTESession Session;
		Session.Start(Config, Random);
		Session.Advance(2.1f);
		TestTrue(TEXT("Choice time out: part 1 starts"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Sequence);
		TestEqual(TEXT("Nothing picked"), Session.ChosenIndex, -1);
		Session.PressKey(EGBQTEKey::Left);
		TestEqual(TEXT("A late option key is part 1's input now"), Session.SequenceResults.Num(), 1);
		TestEqual(TEXT("and not a choice"), Session.ChosenIndex, -1);
		TestEqual(TEXT("No choice in the result"), Session.GetResult().Choice, -1);
	}
	{
		FRandomStream Random(11);
		FGBQTESession Session;
		Session.Start(GBQTETest::MakeConfig(), Random);
		TestTrue(TEXT("No options: no Choice step"), Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Sequence);
	}
	return true;
}

88: void UGBSpellbookComponent::InitializeFromData()
89: {
90: 	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
91: 	if (!Data)
92: 	{
93: 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spellbook: no data subsystem, starting with no spells."));
94: 		return;
95: 	}
96: 
97: 	CancelSustained();
98: 	RemoveAllPassives();

100: 	Loadout.Init(NAME_None, NumSlots);
101: 	CooldownEnd.Reset();
102: 	CooldownLength.Reset();
103: 
104: 	for (const FGBSpellDefinition& Definition : Data->GetSpells())
105: 	{
106: 		if (Definition.bStartsKnown)
107: 		{
108: 			LearnSpellInternal(Definition.Id, false);
109: 		}
110: 	}
111: 
112: 	const FGBPlayerDefinition& Player = Data->GetPlayer();
113: 	for (const FName SpellId : Player.StartingSpells)
114: 	{
115: 		LearnSpellInternal(SpellId, false);
116: 	}
117: 
118: 	EnchantSpellId = Player.EnchantSpell;
119: 	EnchantElement = Player.StartingEnchantElement;
120: 	if (!EnchantSpellId.IsNone())
121: 	{

123: 	}
124: 	else
125: 	{
126: 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spellbook: Player.json has no EnchantSpell; the enchant slot is empty."));
127: 	}
128: 
129: 	int32 Slot = 0;
130: 	for (const FName SpellId : Player.StartingLoadout)
131: 	{
132: 		if (Slot >= NumSlots)
133: 		{
134: 			break;
135: 		}
136: 		if (IsEquippable(SpellId))
137: 		{
138: 			Loadout[Slot++] = SpellId;
139: 		}
140: 		else
141: 		{
142: 			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spellbook: starting loadout spell '%s' is unknown, not known or not equippable; skipped."), *SpellId.ToString());
143: 		}
144: 	}
145: 
315: 	Loadout[Slot] = SpellId;
316: 	OnLoadoutChanged.Broadcast();
317: 	return true;
318: }

323: 	return Keys[FMath::Clamp(Index, 0, NumEnchantChoices - 1)];

343: bool UGBSpellbookComponent::IsBusy() const
344: {
345: 	if (!PendingSpellId.IsNone())
346: 	{
347: 		return true;
348: 	}
349: 	if (const UGBQTEComponent* QTE = GetOwnerQTE())
350: 	{
351: 		if (QTE->IsActive())
352: 		{
353: 			return true;
354: 		}
355: 	}
356: 	return IsValid(ActiveSustained) && ActiveSustained->IsSustaining();
357: }
358: 
359: EGBCastFailReason UGBSpellbookComponent::CanCast(FName SpellId) const
360: {
361: 	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
362: 	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
363: 	if (!Definition || Definition->bPassive)
364: 	{
365: 		return EGBCastFailReason::UnknownSpell;
380: 	}
381: 	if (GetCooldownRemaining(SpellId) > 0.f)
382: 	{
383: 		return EGBCastFailReason::OnCooldown;
384: 	}
385: 	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes();
386: 	const float Cost = GetManaCost(SpellId);
387: 	if (Cost > 0.f && (!Attributes || !Attributes->HasMana(Cost)))
388: 	{
389: 		return EGBCastFailReason::NotEnoughMana;
390: 	}
391: 	return EGBCastFailReason::None;
392: }
393: 
394: bool UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastSlot(int32 Slot)
395: {
396: 	const FName SpellId = GetSlotSpell(Slot);
397: 	if (!SpellId.IsNone() && IsValid(ActiveSustained) && ActiveSustained->GetSpellCast().SpellId == SpellId)
398: 	{
399: 		ActiveSustained->OnCastReleased();
400: 		return true;
401: 	}
402: 	if (SpellId.IsNone())
403: 	{
404: 		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(NAME_None, EGBCastFailReason::EmptySlot);
405: 		return false;
406: 	}
407: 	return TryCastSpell(SpellId);
408: }
409: 
410: bool UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastEnchant()
411: {
412: 	if (EnchantSpellId.IsNone())
413: 	{
414: 		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(NAME_None, EGBCastFailReason::UnknownSpell);
415: 		return false;
416: 	}
417: 	return TryCastSpell(EnchantSpellId);
418: }
419: 
420: bool UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastSpell(FName SpellId)
421: {
422: 	const EGBCastFailReason Reason = CanCast(SpellId);
423: 	if (Reason != EGBCastFailReason::None)
424: 	{
425: 		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, Reason);
426: 		return false;
427: 	}
428: 	return BeginCast(SpellId);
429: }
430: 
431: bool UGBSpellbookComponent::BeginCast(FName SpellId)
432: {
433: 	// Casting with the shield up is not allowed by the character; this only makes sure it is down during the QTE.
434: 	SetBlockHeld(false);
435: 
436: 	const float Cost = GetManaCost(SpellId);
437: 	UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes();
438: 	if (Cost > 0.f && (!Attributes || !Attributes->SpendMana(Cost)))
439: 	{
440: 		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, EGBCastFailReason::NotEnoughMana);
441: 		return false;
442: 	}
443: 
444: 	UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment();
445: 	if (Equipment)
446: 	{
447: 		Equipment->SetSwitchLocked(true);
448: 	}
449: 	PendingSpellId = SpellId;
450: 
451: 	UGBQTEComponent* QTE = GetOwnerQTE();
452: 	if (!QTE)
453: 	{
454: 		// No QTE on the owner: cast at default strength, as when the QTE is switched off.
455: 		FGBQTEResult Result;
456: 		Result.bSuccess = true;
457: 		Result.bUsedQTE = false;
458: 		FinishCast(SpellId, Result);
459: 		return true;
460: 	}
461: 
462: 	// The weapon enchant's QTE opens with the element choice, on fixed keys.
463: 	TArray<EGBQTEKey> ChoiceKeys;
464: 	if (SpellId == EnchantSpellId)
465: 	{
466: 		for (int32 i = 0; i < NumEnchantChoices; ++i)
467: 		{
468: 			ChoiceKeys.Add(GetEnchantChoiceKey(i));
469: 		}
470: 	}
471: 	// The delegate may fire before StartQTE returns (QTE off).
472: 	if (!QTE->StartQTE(SpellId, FGBOnQTEFinished::CreateUObject(this, &UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleQTEFinished, SpellId), ChoiceKeys))
473: 	{
474: 		PendingSpellId = NAME_None;
475: 		if (Equipment)
476: 		{
477: 			Equipment->SetSwitchLocked(false);
478: 		}
479: 		if (Attributes && Cost > 0.f)
480: 		{
481: 			Attributes->RestoreMana(Cost);
482: 		}
483: 		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, EGBCastFailReason::Busy);
484: 		return false;
485: 	}
486: 	return true;
487: }
488: 
489: void UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleQTEFinished(const FGBQTEResult& Result, FName SpellId)
490: {
491: 	// The element picked in the enchant's QTE becomes the enchant element (also for the next casts). Nothing picked
492: 	// (time ran out, or the QTE is off) keeps the element the player had.
493: 	if (SpellId == EnchantSpellId && Result.Choice >= 0 && Result.Choice < NumEnchantChoices)
494: 	{
495: 		SetEnchantElement(GetEnchantChoiceElement(Result.Choice));
496: 	}
497: 	FinishCast(SpellId, Result);
498: }
499: 
500: void UGBSpellbookComponent::FinishCast(FName SpellId, const FGBQTEResult& Result)
501: {
502: 	PendingSpellId = NAME_None;
503: 	if (UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment())
504: 	{
505: 		Equipment->SetSwitchLocked(false);
506: 	}
507: 
508: 	// The owner died while the QTE was running (or the cast outlived it): never spawn from a dead caster.
509: 	bool bOwnerDead = false;
510: 	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner()))
511: 	{
512: 		bOwnerDead = Player->IsDead();
513: 	}
514: 	else if (const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes())
515: 	{
516: 		bOwnerDead = Attributes->IsDead();
517: 	}
518: 	if (bOwnerDead)
519: 	{
520: 		return;
521: 	}
522: 
523: 	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
524: 	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
525: 	if (!Definition)
526: 	{
527: 		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, EGBCastFailReason::UnknownSpell);
528: 		return;
529: 	}
530: 
531: 	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetOwnerStats();
532: 	const float FullCooldown = GBSpell::ResolveParam(*Definition, GBStat::Cooldown, Result.Power, Result.Reach, Result.bUsedQTE, Stats, 0.f);
533: 
534: 	if (!Result.bSuccess)
535: 	{
536: 		// Fizzle: every QTE step was missed. Part of the spent mana is refunded and the cooldown is scaled (QTE.json).
537: 		float RefundFraction = 0.f;
538: 		float CooldownMult = 1.f;
539: 		if (UGBQTEComponent* QTE = GetOwnerQTE())
540: 		{
541: 			RefundFraction = FMath::Clamp(QTE->GetFailManaRefund(), 0.f, 1.f);
542: 			CooldownMult = FMath::Max(0.f, QTE->GetFailCooldownMult());
543: 		}
544: 		StartCooldown(SpellId, FullCooldown * CooldownMult);
545: 		if (RefundFraction > 0.f)
546: 		{
547: 			if (UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes())
548: 			{
549: 				Attributes->RestoreMana(GetManaCost(SpellId) * RefundFraction);
550: 			}
551: 		}
552: 		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, EGBCastFailReason::QTEFailed);
553: 		return;
554: 	}
555: 
556: 	StartCooldown(SpellId, FullCooldown);
557: 
558: 	FGBSpellCast SpellCast = GBSpell::ResolveCast(*Definition, Result.Power, Result.Reach, Result.bUsedQTE, Stats);
559: 	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
560: 	SpellCast.Caster = Owner;
561: 	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(Owner))
562: 	{
563: 		SpellCast.Origin = Player->GetAimOrigin();
564: 		SpellCast.Direction = Player->GetAimDirection();
565: 	}
566: 	else if (Owner)
567: 	{
568: 		FRotator EyeRotation;
569: 		Owner->GetActorEyesViewPoint(SpellCast.Origin, EyeRotation);
570: 		SpellCast.Direction = EyeRotation.Vector();
571: 	}
572: 	if (SpellId == EnchantSpellId)
573: 	{
574: 		SpellCast.Element = EnchantElement;
575: 	}
576: 
577: 	if (Definition->ActorClass.IsEmpty())
578: 	{
579: 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("Spellbook: spell '%s' has no ActorClass in Spells.json."), *SpellId.ToString());
580: 		return;
581: 	}
582: 	UClass* BehaviorClass = LoadClass<AGBSpellActor>(nullptr, *Definition->ActorClass);
583: 	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
584: 	if (!BehaviorClass || !World)
585: 	{
586: 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("Spellbook: spell '%s' ActorClass '%s' is not a GBSpellActor class."), *SpellId.ToString(), *Definition->ActorClass);
587: 		return;
588: 	}
589: 
590: 	const FTransform SpawnTransform(SpellCast.Direction.Rotation(), SpellCast.Origin);
591: 	AGBSpellActor* SpellActor = World->SpawnActorDeferred<AGBSpellActor>(BehaviorClass, SpawnTransform, Owner,
592: 		Cast<APawn>(Owner), ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn);
593: 	if (!SpellActor)
594: 	{
595: 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("Spellbook: could not spawn the behavior of spell '%s'."), *SpellId.ToString());
596: 		return;
597: 	}
598: 	SpellActor->InitSpell(SpellCast);
599: 	SpellActor->FinishSpawning(SpawnTransform);
600: 
675: 	bBlockHeld = false;
676: 	if (IsValid(Current))
677: 	{
678: 		Current->SetRaised(false);
679: 		Current->OnCastReleased();
680: 	}
681: }

683: bool UGBSpellbookComponent::CanRaiseBlock() const
684: {
685: 	if (!PendingSpellId.IsNone())
686: 	{
687: 		return false;
688: 	}
689: 	if (const UGBQTEComponent* QTE = GetOwnerQTE())
690: 	{
691: 		if (QTE->IsActive())
692: 		{
693: 			return false;
694: 		}
695: 	}
696: 	if (const UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment())
697: 	{
698: 		if (Equipment->IsHoldingGun())
699: 		{
700: 			return false;
701: 		}
702: 	}
703: 	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes();
704: 	return !(Attributes && Attributes->IsDead());
705: }

178: void AGBPlayerCharacter::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
179: {
180: 	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
181: 
182: 	UpdateFocusedInteractable();
183: 
184: 	if (IsQTEActive())
185: 	{
186: 		// GDD 7: the movement keys are busy with the QTE, so the last movement input is held.
187: 		if (CanUseGameplayInput() && !bDashing && !LastMoveInput.IsNearlyZero())
188: 		{
189: 			ApplyMoveInput(LastMoveInput);
190: 		}
191: 	}
192: 	else if (GFrameCounter > LastMoveInputFrame + 1)
193: 	{
194: 		// No Move event for a frame: the stick / keys were released (also covers releases during a QTE).
195: 		LastMoveInput = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
196: 	}
197: 
198: 	if (bDashing)
199: 	{
200: 		DashTimeLeft -= DeltaSeconds;
201: 		if (DashTimeLeft <= 0.f)
202: 		{
203: 			EndDash();
204: 		}
205: 		else if (UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement())

606: void AGBPlayerCharacter::HandleDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
607: {
608: 	SetControlEnabled(false);

614: 	}
615: 	else
745: 	Snapshot.Health = Attributes->GetHealth();
746: 	Snapshot.Mana = Attributes->GetMana();
747: 
748: 	Equipment->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
749: 	Spellbook->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
750: 	Inventory->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
751: 	SkillTree->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);

754: void AGBPlayerCharacter::LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
755: {	if (QTE->IsActive())
756: 	{
757: 		QTE->CancelQTE();
758: 	}
759: 	EndDash();
760: 
761: 	SetActorTransform(Snapshot.Transform, false, nullptr, ETeleportType::TeleportPhysics);
762: 	if (Controller)
763: 	{
764: 		Controller->SetControlRotation(Snapshot.ControlRotation);
765: 	}
766: 	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
767: 	{
768: 		Era->SetEraImmediate(Snapshot.Era);
769: 	}
770: 
771: 	// Components first: armor and skill nodes change MaxHealth / MaxMana, which clamp the values set below.
772: 	Equipment->LoadFromSnapshot(Snapshot);
773: 	Spellbook->LoadFromSnapshot(Snapshot);
774: 	Inventory->LoadFromSnapshot(Snapshot);
775: 	SkillTree->LoadFromSnapshot(Snapshot);
776: 
777: 	Attributes->RestoreAll(); // revives
778: 	const float Health = Snapshot.Health > 0.f ? Snapshot.Health : Attributes->GetMaxHealth();
779: 	Attributes->SetHealthAndMana(Health, Snapshot.Mana);
780: 
781: 	UnfreezeMovement();
782: 	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement())
783: 	{
784: 		MoveComp->StopMovementImmediately();
785: 	}
786: 	PendingRecoil = FRotator::ZeroRotator;
787: 	LastMoveInput = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
788: 	SetControlEnabled(true);
789: 	ApplyMovementStats();
790: 	ViewShakeTimeLeft = 0.f;
791: 	ViewShakeDuration = 0.f;
792: 	ViewShakeAmplitudeDeg = 0.f;
793: 	if (Camera)
794: 	{
795: 		Camera->SetRelativeRotation(FRotator::ZeroRotator);
796: 	}
797: 
798: 	if (AGBPlayerController* PC = Cast<AGBPlayerController>(Controller))
799: 	{
800: 		if (PC->GetOpenMenu() == EGBMenu::Death)
801: 		{
802: 			PC->SetMenu(EGBMenu::None);
803: 		}
804: 	}
805: }

938: void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputMove(const FInputActionValue& Value)
939: {

965: void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputLook(const FInputActionValue& Value)
966: {
967: 	if (!CanUseGameplayInput())
968: 	{
969: 		return;
970: 	}
971: 	const FVector2D Delta = Value.Get<FVector2D>(); // mouse: x right, y up
972: 	if (IsQTEActive())
973: 	{
974: 		// Camera locked; the mouse draws the gesture in screen space (y down).
975: 		QTE->HandleGestureInput(FVector2D(Delta.X, -Delta.Y) / MouseUnitsPerPixel);
976: 		return;
977: 	}
978: 	AddLookDelta(static_cast<float>(Delta.X) * ReadStat(GBStat::LookSensitivity, 2.5f), static_cast<float>(Delta.Y) * ReadStat(GBStat::LookSensitivity, 2.5f));
979: }
980: 
981: void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputLookStick(const FInputActionValue& Value)
982: {
983: 	if (!CanUseGameplayInput())
984: 	{
985: 		return;
986: 	}
987: 	const FVector2D Stick = Value.Get<FVector2D>(); // x right, y up
988: 	if (IsQTEActive())
989: 	{
990: 		// Undilated: the world runs at 0.1 speed during the QTE, the gesture runs in real time.
991: 		const float Scale = QTE->GetStickGestureScale();
992: 		const float RealDelta = static_cast<float>(FApp::GetDeltaTime());
993: 		QTE->HandleGestureInput(FVector2D(Stick.X, -Stick.Y) * Scale * RealDelta);
994: 		return;
995: 	}
996: 	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
997: 	// Real time: the QTE runs in real time while the world is slowed.
998: 	const float RealDelta = World ? World->DeltaRealTimeSeconds : 0.f;
999: 	AddLookDelta(static_cast<float>(Stick.X) * ReadStat(GBStat::StickYawRate, 200.f) * RealDelta, static_cast<float>(Stick.Y) * ReadStat(GBStat::StickPitchRate, 140.f) * RealDelta);
1000: }
1001: 
1002: void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputFace(EGBQTEKey Key)
1003: {
1004: 	if (!CanUseGameplayInput())
1005: 	{
1006: 		return;
1007: 	}
1008: 	if (IsQTEActive())
1009: 	{
1010: 		QTE->HandleKey(Key);
1011: 		return;
1012: 	}
1013: 	// GDD 9: hold L2 + Cross, Circle, Square, Triangle = spell slots 2-5 with the staff.
1014: 	if (bSecondaryHeld && Equipment->IsHoldingStaff())
1015: 	{
1016: 		// L2 also raises a summoned shield (Block). A face button while it is up means "cast": lower it first,
1017: 		// otherwise slots 2-5 would be unusable on gamepad for the whole life of the shield.
1018: 		if (IsBlocking())
1019: 		{
1020: 			Spellbook->SetBlockHeld(false);
1021: 		}
1022: 		int32 Slot = 1;
1023: 		switch (Key)
1024: 		{
1025: 		case EGBQTEKey::Down:  Slot = 1; break;
1026: 		case EGBQTEKey::Right: Slot = 2; break;
1027: 		case EGBQTEKey::Left:  Slot = 3; break;
1028: 		case EGBQTEKey::Up:    Slot = 4; break;
1029: 		}
1030: 		Spellbook->TryCastSlot(Slot);
1031: 		return;
1032: 	}
1033: 	switch (Key)
1034: 	{
1035: 	case EGBQTEKey::Down:  InputJump(); break;
1036: 	case EGBQTEKey::Right: InputDash(); break;
1037: 	case EGBQTEKey::Left:  InputReload(); break;
1038: 	case EGBQTEKey::Up:    InputInteract(); break;
1039: 	}
1040: }

1295: 	}

// Runs the spell QTE on the player: slows time, routes input into FGBQTESession, reports the result.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "Components/ActorComponent.h"
#include "QTE/GBQTELogic.h"
#include "GBQTEComponent.generated.h"

DECLARE_DELEGATE_OneParam(FGBOnQTEFinished, const FGBQTEResult& /*Result*/);

/** Extra QTE.json "Stats" keys (recognizer thresholds), read through the owner's stats like the GBStat::QTE* ones. */
namespace GBQTEStat
{
	inline const FName GestureMinScore(TEXT("QTE.GestureMinScore"));             // a gesture below this counts as missed
	inline const FName GestureMinLength(TEXT("QTE.GestureMinLength"));           // pixels before a stroke is judged
	inline const FName GestureIdleSubmitTime(TEXT("QTE.GestureIdleSubmitTime")); // real seconds without input -> auto-submit
	inline const FName LineSubmitLengthMult(TEXT("QTE.LineSubmitLengthMult"));   // line auto-submits at GestureMinLength * this
	inline const FName StickGestureScale(TEXT("QTE.StickGestureScale"));         // right stick: pixels per second at full tilt
	inline const FName FailManaRefund(TEXT("QTE.FailManaRefund"));               // share of the mana cost given back when the spell fizzles (0 = no refund)
	inline const FName FailCooldownMult(TEXT("QTE.FailCooldownMult"));           // the cooldown of a fizzled spell is multiplied by this (1 = full cooldown)
	inline const FName ChoiceTimeLimit(TEXT("QTE.ChoiceTimeLimit"));             // real seconds for a Choice step (the enchant's element)
}

/**
 * GDD 7 requirements handled here:
 *  - time slows to the QTE.TimeDilation stat (read through the owner's UGBStatsComponent, so gear can change it);
 *  - the 3 s limit (QTE.TimeLimit) is real time and covers both parts;
 *  - it can be switched off (QTE.json "bEnabled": false, or console "gb.QTE 0"): StartQTE then finishes at once
 *    with bUsedQTE = false and the spell casts at its default strength.
 * The player character asks IsActive() to lock the camera, hold the last movement input and send
 * W/A/S/D + mouse / face buttons + right stick here instead of to movement and look.
 */
UCLASS(ClassGroup = (Glyphbound), meta = (BlueprintSpawnableComponent))
class GLYPHBOUND_API UGBQTEComponent : public UActorComponent
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	UGBQTEComponent();

	/**
	 * Starts a QTE for SpellId. OnFinished fires exactly once (possibly before this returns, when the QTE is off). Returns
	 * false if one is already running. ChoiceKeys, when given, adds a first step that picks one option per key (the
	 * weapon enchant's element); the result's Choice says which (-1 = none, or the QTE is off).
	 */
	bool StartQTE(FName SpellId, FGBOnQTEFinished OnFinished, const TArray<EGBQTEKey>& ChoiceKeys = TArray<EGBQTEKey>());

	/** Ends the running QTE now; unfinished steps count as missed (death, cutscene). */
	void CancelQTE();

	bool IsActive() const { return bActive; }
	bool IsEnabled() const;
	FName GetSpellId() const { return ActiveSpellId; }

	// ---- Input (from the player character) ----
	void HandleKey(EGBQTEKey Key);
	/** Mouse delta in pixels, or right-stick value * a per-second scale, for part 2. */
	void HandleGestureInput(const FVector2D& Delta);

	/** The running session, for the HUD. Only meaningful while IsActive(). */
	const FGBQTESession& GetSession() const { return Session; }

	/** Result of the last finished QTE (also passed to its delegate). */
	const FGBQTEResult& GetLastResult() const { return LastResult; }

	/** QTE.StickGestureScale: the player character sends right-stick value * this * real delta seconds to HandleGestureInput. */
	float GetStickGestureScale() const;
	/** Share of the mana cost refunded when the spell fizzles (QTE.FailManaRefund, 0 = no refund). */
	float GetFailManaRefund() const;
	/** Multiplier on the cooldown of a fizzled spell (QTE.FailCooldownMult, 1 = full cooldown, 0 = no cooldown). */
	float GetFailCooldownMult() const;

	virtual void TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction) override;

protected:
	virtual void EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason) override;

	void Finish();

	/** QTE.json "Stats"[Key] (Default when missing, with a warning), then the owner's modifiers for Key. */
	float ReadStat(FName Key, float Default) const;
	FGBQTEConfig BuildConfig() const;

	FGBQTESession Session;
	FGBQTEResult LastResult;
	/** Read at StartQTE so gear changes apply to the next QTE. */
	float GestureIdleSubmitTime = 0.f;	float LineSubmitLengthMult = 0.f;
	FGBOnQTEFinished FinishedDelegate;
	FName ActiveSpellId = NAME_None;
	bool bActive = false;
	/** The next Finish ends a CancelQTE: its result is forced to unsuccessful, whatever the score so far. */
	bool bForceFailResult = false;
	/** Real seconds without gesture input, to auto-submit a stroke when the hand pauses. */
	float GestureIdleTime = 0.f;
	FRandomStream Random;
};
// The player's spells: what is known, the 5-slot loadout, the fixed enchant slot, mana, cooldowns, casting.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "Components/ActorComponent.h"
#include "Spells/GBSpellTypes.h"
#include "QTE/GBQTELogic.h"
#include "GBSpellbookComponent.generated.h"

class AGBSpellActor;
class AGBSpell_Shield;
class AGBHeldItem;
class UGBStatsComponent;
class UGBAttributeComponent;
class UGBEquipmentComponent;
class UGBQTEComponent;
struct FGBPlayerSnapshot;
struct FGBQTEResult;

DECLARE_MULTICAST_DELEGATE_OneParam(FGBOnSpellCast, const FGBSpellCast& /*Cast*/);
DECLARE_MULTICAST_DELEGATE_TwoParams(FGBOnCastFailed, FName /*SpellId*/, EGBCastFailReason /*Reason*/);

/**
 * Cast flow (GDD 6 and 7):
 *   TryCastSlot / TryCastEnchant -> checks (held item, known, cooldown, mana, not busy)
 *   -> mana is spent now ("mana is always spent, even when the spell fails")
 *   -> the owner's UGBQTEComponent runs (or returns at once when the QTE is off)
 *   -> if at least one QTE step was right: ResolveCast + spawn the behavior actor; cooldown starts either way.
 * Rules: spells need the staff in hand; the enchant needs a gun in hand; air passives are never cast, they just
 * apply their modifiers while known.
 */
UCLASS(ClassGroup = (Glyphbound), meta = (BlueprintSpawnableComponent))
class GLYPHBOUND_API UGBSpellbookComponent : public UActorComponent
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	static constexpr int32 NumSlots = 5;

	UGBSpellbookComponent();

	/** Learns the starting spells and loadout from Player.json / Spells.json. Called by the player on BeginPlay. */
	void InitializeFromData();

	// ---- Knowledge ----
	bool KnowsSpell(FName SpellId) const;
	/** Returns true if it was new. Passives start applying their modifiers at once. */
	bool LearnSpell(FName SpellId);
	/** Learns every spell whose UnlockGroup matches (the knowledge of an elemental zone). Returns how many were new. */
	int32 LearnGroup(FName UnlockGroup);
	const TArray<FName>& GetKnownSpells() const { return KnownSpells; }

	// ---- Loadout ----
	/** NAME_None for an empty slot. Slot 0..NumSlots-1. */
	FName GetSlotSpell(int32 Slot) const;
	/** Only known, non-passive, non-enchant spells. A spell already in another slot is moved. NAME_None clears the slot. */
	bool EquipSpell(int32 Slot, FName SpellId);
	FName GetEnchantSpell() const { return EnchantSpellId; }
	EGBElement GetEnchantElement() const { return EnchantElement; }
	void SetEnchantElement(EGBElement InElement);
	/**
	 * The weapon enchant's QTE starts with the element choice (owner, Nov 13), always in the same places:
	 * A / Square = fire, W / Triangle = lightning, D / Circle = ice. Index i of one goes with index i of the other.
	 */
	static constexpr int32 NumEnchantChoices = 3;
	static EGBQTEKey GetEnchantChoiceKey(int32 Index);
	static EGBElement GetEnchantChoiceElement(int32 Index);

	// ---- Casting ----
	/** Slot 0 = right click / L1, slots 1-4 = keys 1-4. If a sustained spell of that slot is running, this ends it instead. */
	bool TryCastSlot(int32 Slot);
	bool TryCastEnchant();
	/** Why the spell could not be cast right now; None if it can. */
	EGBCastFailReason CanCast(FName SpellId) const;
	/** A QTE is running or a sustained spell is active. */
	bool IsBusy() const;
	/** Ends the active sustained spell, if any (weapon switch, death). */
	void CancelSustained();

	// ---- Summoned shield (GDD 6: the ice shield is a held block, like a first-person RPG shield) ----
	// Casting a shield spell summons the shield for its Duration; it does nothing by itself. The player raises it
	// by holding the Block input (Q / L2 with the staff or the sword in hand) and it only blocks while raised.
	// A summoned shield does not make the spellbook busy: other spells can be cast while it is lowered.
	/** Called by a shield behavior when it starts / ends. A new shield replaces the previous one (which ends). */
	void RegisterShield(AGBSpellActor* Shield);
	void UnregisterShield(AGBSpellActor* Shield);
	/** The Block input. Ignored (and forced to false) when there is no shield or the held item is a gun. */
	void SetBlockHeld(bool bHeld);
	bool HasShield() const;
	bool IsBlockRaised() const;
	/** 0..1 of the shield's remaining health, and seconds until it expires; 0 when there is no shield. */
	float GetShieldHealthFraction() const;
	float GetShieldTimeRemaining() const;
	/** The summoned shield (null if none), e.g. for the HUD to read its spell's Strings (Sprite, Color). */
	AGBSpellActor* GetShieldActor() const;

	/** Seconds left / total, for the HUD. */
	float GetCooldownRemaining(FName SpellId) const;
	float GetCooldownDuration(FName SpellId) const;
	/** Mana cost right now (with modifiers, at default strength). */
	float GetManaCost(FName SpellId) const;

	void SaveToSnapshot(FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot) const;
	void LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot);

	FGBOnSpellCast OnSpellCast;
	FGBOnCastFailed OnCastFailed;
	FSimpleMulticastDelegate OnLoadoutChanged;

protected:
	virtual void BeginPlay() override;
	virtual void EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason) override;

	bool BeginCast(FName SpellId);
	void FinishCast(FName SpellId, const FGBQTEResult& Result);

	/** QTE delegate target: delegate payloads come after the delegate's own params, so this forwards to FinishCast. */
	void HandleQTEFinished(const FGBQTEResult& Result, FName SpellId);
	/** Common path of TryCastSlot / TryCastEnchant: checks, broadcasts the failure reason, or begins the cast. */
	bool TryCastSpell(FName SpellId);
	void HandleSustainedEnded();
	void HandleActiveItemChanged(AGBHeldItem* NewItem);
	void HandleOwnerDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow);

	bool LearnSpellInternal(FName SpellId, bool bAutoEquip);
	/** Known, defined, castable, not passive, not the enchant. */
	bool IsEquippable(FName SpellId) const;
	void StartCooldown(FName SpellId, float Seconds);

	static FName PassiveSource(FName SpellId);
	void ApplyPassive(FName SpellId);
	void RemoveAllPassives();
	void ReapplyPassives();

	UGBStatsComponent* GetOwnerStats() const;
	UGBAttributeComponent* GetOwnerAttributes() const;
	UGBEquipmentComponent* GetOwnerEquipment() const;
	UGBQTEComponent* GetOwnerQTE() const;
	double GetNow() const;

	FDelegateHandle DataReloadedHandle;
	FDelegateHandle ActiveItemChangedHandle;
	FDelegateHandle DeathHandle;

	UPROPERTY() TArray<FName> KnownSpells;
	UPROPERTY() TArray<FName> Loadout;
	UPROPERTY() FName EnchantSpellId = NAME_None;
	UPROPERTY() EGBElement EnchantElement = EGBElement::Fire;
	/** Spell id -> world time when it is ready again. */
	TMap<FName, double> CooldownEnd;
	TMap<FName, float> CooldownLength;
	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<AGBSpellActor> ActiveSustained;
	FName PendingSpellId = NAME_None;

	/** The summoned shield, if any (it ends itself: Duration, broken, death). */
	TWeakObjectPtr<AGBSpell_Shield> Shield;
	/** Block input accepted and the shield raised (forced false when refused). */
	bool bBlockHeld = false;
	/** The shield, if it is still active. */
	AGBSpell_Shield* GetActiveShield() const;
	/** Block is refused with a gun in hand, while a cast / QTE runs, and while dead. */
	bool CanRaiseBlock() const;
	/** Lowers and ends the summoned shield now (death, teardown). */
	void EndShield();
};

#include "Player/GBInputSet.h"

#include "InputAction.h"
#include "InputMappingContext.h"
#include "InputModifiers.h"
#include "InputCoreTypes.h"

namespace
{
	// Stick dead zones (input feel, not gameplay numbers).
	constexpr float GameplayStickDeadZone = 0.2f;
	constexpr float MenuStickDeadZone = 0.5f;

	/** Where a digital key pushes a 2D axis. */
	enum class EDir2D : uint8 { Right, Left, Up, Down };

	UInputAction* MakeAction(UObject* Outer, const TCHAR* Name, EInputActionValueType Type, bool bTriggerWhenPaused = false)
	{
		UInputAction* Action = NewObject<UInputAction>(Outer, MakeUniqueObjectName(Outer, UInputAction::StaticClass(), FName(Name)));
		Action->ValueType = Type;
		Action->bTriggerWhenPaused = bTriggerWhenPaused;
		return Action;
	}

	UInputModifierNegate* MakeNegate(UObject* Outer)
	{
		return NewObject<UInputModifierNegate>(Outer);
	}

	UInputModifierSwizzleAxis* MakeSwizzleYXZ(UObject* Outer)
	{
		UInputModifierSwizzleAxis* Swizzle = NewObject<UInputModifierSwizzleAxis>(Outer);
		Swizzle->Order = EInputAxisSwizzle::YXZ;
		return Swizzle;
	}

	UInputModifierDeadZone* MakeDeadZone(UObject* Outer, float Lower)
	{
		UInputModifierDeadZone* DeadZone = NewObject<UInputModifierDeadZone>(Outer);
		DeadZone->LowerThreshold = Lower;
		DeadZone->UpperThreshold = 1.f;
		DeadZone->Type = EDeadZoneType::Radial;
		return DeadZone;
	}

	// MapKey returns a reference into the context's array: every helper adds its modifiers before the next MapKey.

	void MapButton(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key)
	{
		Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
	}

	/** Axis1D: +1 or -1. */
	void MapAxis1D(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, bool bNegative = false)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		if (bNegative)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeNegate(Context));
		}
	}

	/** Axis2D from a digital key: x = right, y = up / forward. */
	void MapDir2D(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, EDir2D Dir)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		if (Dir == EDir2D::Up || Dir == EDir2D::Down)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeSwizzleYXZ(Context));
		}
		if (Dir == EDir2D::Left || Dir == EDir2D::Down)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeNegate(Context));
		}
	}

	/** Axis2D from a stick, with a radial dead zone. */
	void MapStick(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, float DeadZone)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeDeadZone(Context, DeadZone));
	}

	void MapWASD(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action)
	{
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::W, EDir2D::Up);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::S, EDir2D::Down);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::A, EDir2D::Left);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::D, EDir2D::Right);
	}
}

void UGBInputSet::Build()
{
	if (GameplayContext)
	{
		return; // built once per player
	}

	using EType = EInputActionValueType;

	// ---- Actions ----
	Move = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Move"), EType::Axis2D);
	Look = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Look"), EType::Axis2D);
	LookStick = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_LookStick"), EType::Axis2D);
	SwitchWeapon = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SwitchWeapon"), EType::Axis1D);
	CycleQuickUse = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_CycleQuickUse"), EType::Axis1D);

	Jump = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Jump"), EType::Boolean);
	Dash = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Dash"), EType::Boolean);
	Primary = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Primary"), EType::Boolean);
	Secondary = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Secondary"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot1 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot1"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot2 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot2"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot3 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot3"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot4 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot4"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot5 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot5"), EType::Boolean);
	Enchant = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Enchant"), EType::Boolean);
	Reload = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Reload"), EType::Boolean);
	Interact = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Interact"), EType::Boolean);
	QuickUse = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QuickUse"), EType::Boolean);
	Block = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Block"), EType::Boolean);
	// These three open / close menus, so they must also fire while the game is paused.
	Inventory = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Inventory"), EType::Boolean, true);
	SkillTrees = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SkillTrees"), EType::Boolean, true);
	Pause = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Pause"), EType::Boolean, true);

	FaceUp = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceUp"), EType::Boolean);
	FaceLeft = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceLeft"), EType::Boolean);
	FaceDown = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceDown"), EType::Boolean);
	FaceRight = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceRight"), EType::Boolean);

	QTEUp = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTEUp"), EType::Boolean);
	QTELeft = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTELeft"), EType::Boolean);
	QTEDown = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTEDown"), EType::Boolean);
	QTERight = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTERight"), EType::Boolean);

	MenuNavigate = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuNavigate"), EType::Axis2D, true);
	MenuConfirm = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuConfirm"), EType::Boolean, true);
	MenuBack = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuBack"), EType::Boolean, true);
	MenuTabKey = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuTabKey"), EType::Boolean, true);
	MenuTab = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuTab"), EType::Axis1D, true);
	MenuClick = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuClick"), EType::Boolean, true);
	MenuReset = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuReset"), EType::Boolean, true);

	// ---- Gameplay context (GDD 9) ----
	GameplayContext = NewObject<UInputMappingContext>(this, MakeUniqueObjectName(this, UInputMappingContext::StaticClass(), TEXT("IMC_Gameplay")));
	UInputMappingContext* G = GameplayContext;

	MapWASD(G, Move);
	MapStick(G, Move, EKeys::Gamepad_Left2D, GameplayStickDeadZone);

	G->MapKey(Look, EKeys::Mouse2D);
	MapStick(G, LookStick, EKeys::Gamepad_Right2D, GameplayStickDeadZone);

	MapAxis1D(G, SwitchWeapon, EKeys::MouseWheelAxis);
	MapAxis1D(G, SwitchWeapon, EKeys::LeftAlt);
	MapAxis1D(G, SwitchWeapon, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Up);

	MapAxis1D(G, CycleQuickUse, EKeys::LeftShift);
	MapAxis1D(G, CycleQuickUse, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Right);
	MapAxis1D(G, CycleQuickUse, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Left, true);

	MapButton(G, Jump, EKeys::SpaceBar);
	MapButton(G, Dash, EKeys::LeftControl);

	MapButton(G, Primary, EKeys::LeftMouseButton);
	MapButton(G, Primary, EKeys::Gamepad_RightTrigger);
	MapButton(G, Secondary, EKeys::RightMouseButton);
	MapButton(G, Secondary, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftTrigger);

	MapButton(G, SpellSlot1, EKeys::RightMouseButton);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot1, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftShoulder);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot2, EKeys::One);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot3, EKeys::Two);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot4, EKeys::Three);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot5, EKeys::Four);

	MapButton(G, Enchant, EKeys::T);
	MapButton(G, Enchant, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftShoulder);
	MapButton(G, Reload, EKeys::R);
	MapButton(G, Interact, EKeys::G);
	MapButton(G, QuickUse, EKeys::F);
	MapButton(G, QuickUse, EKeys::Gamepad_RightShoulder);
	// L2 is also Secondary (aim with a gun, spell modifier with the staff): both meanings coexist, the character
	// and the spellbook decide (block is refused with a gun in hand).
	MapButton(G, Block, EKeys::Q);
	MapButton(G, Block, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftTrigger);

	MapButton(G, Inventory, EKeys::Tab);
	MapButton(G, Inventory, EKeys::I);
	MapButton(G, Inventory, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Left);
	MapButton(G, SkillTrees, EKeys::K);
	MapButton(G, Pause, EKeys::Escape);
	MapButton(G, Pause, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Right);

	MapButton(G, FaceUp, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Top);
	MapButton(G, FaceLeft, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Left);
	MapButton(G, FaceDown, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Bottom);
	MapButton(G, FaceRight, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Right);

	MapButton(G, QTEUp, EKeys::W);
	MapButton(G, QTELeft, EKeys::A);
	MapButton(G, QTEDown, EKeys::S);
	MapButton(G, QTERight, EKeys::D);

	// ---- Menu context ----
	MenuContext = NewObject<UInputMappingContext>(this, MakeUniqueObjectName(this, UInputMappingContext::StaticClass(), TEXT("IMC_Menu")));
	UInputMappingContext* M = MenuContext;

	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Up, EDir2D::Up);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Down, EDir2D::Down);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Left, EDir2D::Left);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Right, EDir2D::Right);
	MapWASD(M, MenuNavigate);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Up, EDir2D::Up);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Down, EDir2D::Down);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Left, EDir2D::Left);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Right, EDir2D::Right);
	MapStick(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_Left2D, MenuStickDeadZone);

	MapButton(M, MenuConfirm, EKeys::Enter);
	MapButton(M, MenuConfirm, EKeys::SpaceBar);
	MapButton(M, MenuConfirm, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Bottom);

	MapButton(M, MenuBack, EKeys::Escape);
	// Tab is the inventory's own key: there it closes the inventory even from a gun branch page or a sub-choice
	// (Sol 6.1 review); on the other screens it is still "back".
	MapButton(M, MenuTabKey, EKeys::Tab);
	MapButton(M, MenuBack, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Right);

	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Q, true);
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::E);
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftShoulder, true);
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Gamepad_RightShoulder);
	// L2 also turns pages (owner: "L1 y L2" between the two skill trees). In the gameplay context it is aim / block,
	// but that context is not read while a menu is open.
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftTrigger);

	MapButton(M, MenuClick, EKeys::LeftMouseButton);

	// Skill reset shortcut (menu context only): R and the gamepad top face button. Neither is mapped
	// to another menu action (R is Reload only in the gameplay context; the top face button is only
	// a raw FaceUp gameplay input), so no existing menu binding is changed.
	MapButton(M, MenuReset, EKeys::R);
	MapButton(M, MenuReset, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Top);

	// Inside menus: the gamepad Menu / View buttons, I and K still toggle their screens (Esc is "back"; Tab closes the inventory, "back" elsewhere).
	MapButton(M, Pause, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Right);
	MapButton(M, Inventory, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Left);
	MapButton(M, Inventory, EKeys::I);
	MapButton(M, SkillTrees, EKeys::K);
}
